<div class='question question'>

Write the missing sign $( = , <$ or $>)$ in the box.

$16 \times 7  \ \boxed{\phantom{11}} \ 17 \times 6$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
16 \times 7  &= 112 \\\\
17 \times 6  &= 102 \\\\
16 \times 7  &\boxed{>} 17 \times 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$16 \times 7  \ \boxed{>} \ 17 \times 6$

</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subsubquestion'>
<div class='question subsubquestion'>

Work out the input when the output is $120$.

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let input $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
(a + 7) \times 4 &= 120 \\\\
4 \times a + 4 \times 7 &= 120 \\\\
4a + 28 &= 120 \\\\
4a &= 120 - 28 \\\\
4a &= 92 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{92}{4} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{4 \times 23}{4} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{\cancel 4 \times 23}{\cancel 4} \\\\
a &= 23
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

23

</div>

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

There are two possible function machines that will give the results shown below.

Work out the rules for the two possible machines.

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

**Rule 1**

Let first label $= + a$

Let second label $= \times b$

$
\begin{aligned}
(5 + a) \times b &= 4 \\\\
5b + ab &= 4 \\\\
(8 + a) \times b &= 10 \\\\
8b + ab &= 10 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Lets solve for \'b\'

$
\begin{aligned}
8b + ab &= 10 \\\\
8b + (4 - 5b) &= 10 \\\\
8b - 5b &= 10 - 4 \\\\
3b &= 6 \\\\
b &= \dfrac{6}{3} \\\\
b &= \dfrac{2 \times 3}{3} \\\\
b &= \dfrac{2 \times \cancel 3}{\cancel 3} \\\\
b &= 2
\end{aligned}
$

Lets solve for \'a\'

$
\begin{aligned}
5b + ab &= 4 \\\\
5 \times 2 + 2 \times a &= 4 \\\\
10 + 2a &= 4 \\\\
2a &= 4 - 10 \\\\
2a &= -6 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{-6}{2} \\\\
a &= -3 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Therefore,

The rule $= - 3 \ \text{then}\ \times 2$

**Rule 2**

Let first label $= \times a$

Let second label $= + b$

$
\begin{aligned}
5 \times a + b &= 4 \\\\
5a + b &= 4 \\\\
8 \times a + b &= 10 \\\\
8a + b &= 10 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Lets solve for \'a\'

$
\begin{aligned}
8a + b &= 10 \\\\
8a + (4 - 5a) &= 10 \\\\
8a - 5a &= 10 - 4 \\\\
3a &= 6 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{6}{3} \\\\
a &= 2 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Lets solve for \'b\'

$
\begin{aligned}
5a + b &= 4 \\\\
5 \times 2 + b &= 4 \\\\
10 + b &= 4 \\\\
b &= 4 - 10 \\\\
b &= -6
\end{aligned}
$

Therefore,

The rule $= \times 2 \ \text{then}\ - 6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$-3 \ \text{then}\ \times 2 \\\\ \text{Or,}\ \times 2 \ \text{then}\ - 6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>**Rule 1**

Let first label $= + a$

Let second label $= \times b$

$
\begin{aligned}
(5 + a) \times b &= 4 \\\\
5b + ab &= 4 \\\\
(8 + a) \times b &= 10 \\\\
8b + ab &= 10 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Lets solve for \'b\'

$
\begin{aligned}
8b + ab &= 10 \\\\
8b + (4 - 5b) &= 10 \\\\
8b - 5b &= 10 - 4 \\\\
3b &= 6 \\\\
b &= \dfrac{6}{3} \\\\
b &= \dfrac{2 \times 3}{3} \\\\
b &= \dfrac{2 \times \cancel 3}{\cancel 3} \\\\
b &= 2
\end{aligned}
$

Lets solve for \'a\'

$
\begin{aligned}
5b + ab &= 4 \\\\
5 \times 2 + 2 \times a &= 4 \\\\
10 + 2a &= 4 \\\\
2a &= 4 - 10 \\\\
2a &= -6 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{-6}{2} \\\\
a &= -3 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Therefore,

The rule $= - 3 \ \text{then}\ \times 2$

**Rule 2**

Let first label $= \times a$

Let second label $= + b$

$
\begin{aligned}
5 \times a + b &= 4 \\\\
5a + b &= 4 \\\\
8 \times a + b &= 10 \\\\
8a + b &= 10 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Lets solve for \'a\'

$
\begin{aligned}
8a + b &= 10 \\\\
8a + (4 - 5a) &= 10 \\\\
8a - 5a &= 10 - 4 \\\\
3a &= 6 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{6}{3} \\\\
a &= 2 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Lets solve for \'b\'

$
\begin{aligned}
5a + b &= 4 \\\\
5 \times 2 + b &= 4 \\\\
10 + b &= 4 \\\\
b &= 4 - 10 \\\\
b &= -6
\end{aligned}
$

Therefore,

The rule $= \times 2 \ \text{then}\ - 6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$-3 \ \text{then}\ \times 2 \\\\ \text{Or,}\ \times 2 \ \text{then}\ - 6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

For example, using four of the cards, the largest $4$-digit even number that can be made is $9854$.

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the smallest $5$-digit multiple of $5$ that can be made?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 13485 \\\\
&= \dfrac {13485} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2697 \times 5} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2697 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
&= 2697
\end {aligned}
$

Hence, $13485$ is the smallest $5$ digit number and also a multiple of $5$. 

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$13485$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= 13485 \\\\

&= \dfrac {2697 \times 5} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2697 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
&= 2697
\end {aligned}
$

Hence, $13485$ is the smallest $5$ digit number and also a multiple of $5$. 
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Using five cards, what is the nearest number to $50000$ that can be made?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

The number close to $50000$ are $49853$ and $51348$ \\\\

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Difference with number greater than 50000}  &= 51348 - 50000 \\\\
                                                   &= 1348 \\\\
\text {Difference with number smaller than 50000}  &= 50000 - 49853 \\\\
                                                   &= 147
\end {aligned}
$

Hence, $49853$ is the the nearest number to $50000$.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$49853$

</div>
</div>

\end {aligned}
$

</code></pre>

Without doing any long multiplications, use Sammy’s calculation to help you write down the results of the following:

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$38 \times 42$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 38 \times 42 \\\\
&= (37 + 1) \times 42 \\\\
&= 37 \times 42 + 1 \times 42 \\\\
&= 1554 + 42 \\\\
&= 1596
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1596$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= 38 \times 42 \\\\
&= (37 + 1) \times 42 \\\\
&= 37 \times 42 + 1 \times 42 \\\\
&= 1554 + 42 \\\\
&= 1596
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1596$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$37 \times 21$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 37 \times 21 \\\\
&= 37 \times \dfrac {42} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {37 \times 42} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1554} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {777 \times 2} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {777 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 777
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$777$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= 37 \times 21 \\\\
&= 37 \times \dfrac {42} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {37 \times 42} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1554} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {777 \times 2} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {777 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 777
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$777$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$155.4 \div 42$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 155.4 \div 42 \\\\
&= \dfrac {155.4} {42} \\\\
&= \dfrac {155.4 \times 10} {42 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1554} {42 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {37 \times 42} {42 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {37 \times \cancel {42}} {\cancel {42} \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {37} {10} \\\\
&= 3.7
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3.7$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= 155.4 \div 42 \\\\
&= \dfrac {155.4} {42} \\\\
&= \dfrac {155.4 \times 10} {42 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1554} {42 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {37 \times 42} {42 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {37 \times \cancel {42}} {\cancel {42} \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {37} {10} \\\\
&= 3.7
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$3.7$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Zoya has a jug of capacity $1.5 \ \text{litres}$ which is full of lemonade.

She has eight cups each of capacity $150 \ \text{ml}$.

Zoya fills the cups with lemonade from her jug.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

TODOWORKING

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

TODOANSWER

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What volume of lemonade is left in the jug?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1.5 \text{liter}\ - 150 \text{ml}\ \times 8 \\\\
&= (1.5 \times 1000) \text{ml}\ - 150 \text{ml}\ \times 8 \\\\
&= 1500 - 1200 \\\\
&= 300 \text{ml}\ \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

300 ml

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1.5 \text{liter}\ - 150 \text{ml}\ \times 8 \\\\
&= (1.5 \times 1000) \text{ml}\ - 150 \text{ml}\ \times 8 \\\\
&= 1500 - 1200 \\\\
&= 300 \text{ml}\ \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>300 ml
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Zoya has five $2 \ \text{litre}$ bottles of lemonade

How many times could Zoya completely fill her jug from these bottles?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total volume Zoya has} &= 5 \times 2 \text{litre} \\\\
&= 10 \text{litre} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Let number of times jug can be filled $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 1.5 &= 10 \\\\
a \times \dfrac{15}{10} &= 10 \\\\
a \times \dfrac{3}{2} &= 10 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{10}{\dfrac{3}{2}} \\\\
a &= 10 \times \dfrac{2}{3} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{20}{3} \\\\
a &= 6.66 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Since the jug had to be completely filled,

Total times $= 6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total volume Zoya has} &= 5 \times 2 \text{litre} \\\\
&= 10 \text{litre} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Let number of times jug can be filled $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 1.5 &= 10 \\\\
a \times \dfrac{15}{10} &= 10 \\\\
a \times \dfrac{3}{2} &= 10 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{10}{\dfrac{3}{2}} \\\\
a &= 10 \times \dfrac{2}{3} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{20}{3} \\\\
a &= 6.66 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Since the jug had to be completely filled,

Total times $= 6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-91E92D06-A849-497B-A843-4A8E1C0A3EB7'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which number is *one hundred* less than *three thousand and sixteen*?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3016 - 100 \\\\
&= 2916
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2916$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3016 - 100 \\\\
&= 2916
\end{aligned}
$

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2916$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-407F2714-A43E-4361-B05E-37CC2E87E623'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Write the missing sign $( = , <$ or $>)$ in the box.

$19 \times 3 \ \boxed{\phantom{11}} \  28 \times 2$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{LHS}   &= 19 \times 3 \\\\
             &= 57 \\\\
\text{RHS}   &= 28 \times 2 \\\\
             &= 56 \\\\
\text{LHS}\  &\boxed{>} \ \text{RHS} \\\\
19 \times 3 \ &\boxed{\green
{>}} \ 28 \times 2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$19 \times 3 \ \quad  > \quad  28 \times 2$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{LHS}   &= 19 \times 3 \\\\
             &= 57 \\\\
\text{RHS}   &= 28 \times 2 \\\\
             &= 56 \\\\
\text{LHS}\  &\boxed{>} \ \text{RHS} \\\\
19 \times 3 \ &\boxed{\rtq_katex_color_answer{>}} \ 28 \times 2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$19 \times 3 \ \quad  > \quad  28 \times 2$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-2CD8DF37-1EC5-419D-94FD-BB6B498FD8C3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Lisa thinks of her favourite number.

She multiplies her favourite number by $2$, subtracts $3$ and gets $19$.


What is Lisa’s favourite number?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let Lisa’s favourite number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times a - 3      &= 19 \\\\
2a                  &= 19 + 3 \\\\
2a                  &= 22 \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {22} {2} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {11 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {11 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a                   &= 11
\end{aligned}
$

Lisa’s favourite number $= 11$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$11$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let Lisa’s favourite number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times a - 3      &= 19 \\\\
2a                  &= 19 + 3 \\\\
2a                  &= 22 \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {22} {2} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {11 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {11 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a                   &= 11
\end{aligned}
$

Lisa’s favourite number $= 11$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$11$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Cameron has five number cards.

$\boxed{1} \quad \boxed{2} \quad \boxed{3} \quad \boxed{4} \quad \boxed{5}$

The cards can be placed together to form a number.

For example, using three of his cards Cameron can create the smallest $3$-digit multiple of $3$.

$\boxed{1} \quad \boxed{2} \quad \boxed{3}$

In the questions that follow, *choosing from Cameron’s cards*, write numbers on the blank cards to make:

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$4.25 \times 1.34$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4.25 \times 1.34 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {425 \times 134} {100 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{56950} {10000} \\\\
&= 5.6950
\end{aligned}
$



</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5.6950$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4.25 \times 1.34 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {425 \times 134} {100 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{56950} {10000} \\\\
&= 5.6950
\end{aligned}
$


</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$56950 \div 4.25$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
&= 56950 \div 4.25 \\\\
&= \dfrac {56950} {4.25} \\\\
&= \dfrac {56950 \times 100} {425} \\\\
&= 134 \times 100 \\\\
&= 13400
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$13400$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 56950 \div 4.25 \\\\
&= \dfrac {56950} {4.25} \\\\
&= \dfrac {56950 \times 100} {425} \\\\
&= 134 \times 100 \\\\
&= 13400
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$13400$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$42.5 \times 67$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
&=42.5 \times 67 \\\\
&= \dfrac {425 \times 67} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {425 \times 67 \times 2} {10 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {425 \times 134} {10 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {56950} {10 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5695 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5695} {2} \\\\
&= 2847.5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2847.5$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
&=42.5 \times 67 \\\\
&= \dfrac {425 \times 67} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {425 \times 67 \times 2} {10 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {425 \times 134} {10 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {56950} {10 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5695 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5695} {2} \\\\
&= 2847.5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2847.5$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

Their marks were $13 \quad 18 \quad 14 \quad 20 \quad 7 \quad 18$

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the difference between the highest and lowest marks?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text{Highest marks}    &= 20 \\\\
\text{Lowest marks}     &= 7 \\\\
\text{Difference}       &= 20 - 7 \\\\
                        &= 13
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$13$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text{Highest marks}    &= 20 \\\\
\text{Lowest marks}     &= 7 \\\\
\text{Difference}       &= 20 - 7 \\\\
                        &= 13
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Ashleigh’s mark was seven more than Bella’s mark and six less than Connie’s mark.

What was Ashleigh’s mark?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

This can be inferred by using trial and error.

Marks $7$ and $20$ meet the criteria. Let's verify.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Bella’s marks}       &= 7 \\\\
\text {Ashleigh’s marks}    &= 7 + 7 \\\\
                            &= 14 \\\\
\text {Connie’s marks}      &= 14 + 6 \\\\
                            &= 20
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$14$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>This can be inferred by using trial and error.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Bella’s marks}       &= 7 \\\\
\text {Ashleigh’s marks}    &= 7 + 7 \\\\
                            &= 14 \\\\
\text {Connie’s marks}      &= 14 + 6 \\\\
                            &= 20
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$14$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-4A4E7B81-270C-40B5-B238-65BE74C68BA8'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

In order to convert from the imperial units ounces and pounds to the metric unit kilograms, you should use the following conversions:

$
\begin{aligned}
16 \ \text{ounces} &= 1 \ \text{pound}\\
2.2 \ \text{pounds} &= 1 \ \text{kg}
\end{aligned}
$

Newborn tiger cubs weigh about $56 \ \text{ounces}$.

Circle the mass in kilograms which gives the best approximation of the mass of a newborn tiger cub.

$0.5 \ \text{kg} \quad 1 \ \text{kg} \quad 1.5 \ \text{kg} \quad 2 \ \text{kg} \quad 2.5 \ \text{kg} \quad 3 \ \text{kg}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 56 \ \text{ounces} \\\\
&= (56 \div 16) \ \text{pounds} \\\\
&= 3.5 \ \text{pounds} \\\\
&= (3.5 \div 2.2) \ \text{kg} \\\\
&= 1.59 \ \text{kg} \\\\
&= 1.5 \ \text{kg} \ \text{(approx.)} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
<div class='working'>

TODOWORKING

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

1.5 kg

</div>
<div class='answer'>

TODOANSWER

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 56 \ \text{ounces} \\\\
&= (56 \div 16) \ \text{pounds} \\\\
&= 3.5 \ \text{pounds} \\\\
&= (3.5 \div 2.2) \ \text{kg} \\\\
&= 1.59 \ \text{kg} \\\\
&= 1.5 \ \text{kg} \ \text{(approx.)} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>TODOWORKING
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>1.5 kg
</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C13D92E3-895B-4965-8831-8AD95CB87317'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the difference between the product of $4$ and $11$ and the difference of $4$ and $11$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>



$
\begin{aligned}
&= (4 \times 11) - (4 - 11)  \\\\
&= 44 - (-7) \\\\
&= 44 + 7 \\\\
&= 51
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$51$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= (4 \times 11) - (4 - 11)  \\\\
&= 44 - (-7) \\\\
&= 44 + 7 \\\\
&= 51
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-09B08DAF-540F-4115-B9CB-3DA13E21C2F3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Neil lends Rodger $\pounds 4$. Rodger pays back $30 \%$ of this the next day. How much does he still owe Neil?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>



Let's convert pounds to pence.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total amount} &= \pounds 4 \\\\
&= 4 \times 100 \text{p} \\\\
&= 400 \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$

Rodger pays back $30 \%$. So,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Amount paid} &= 30\% \text{ of } 400 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30} {100} \times 400 \\\\
&= 30 \times 4 \\\\
&= 120  \text{p} 
\end{aligned}
$

Let's calculate the amount Rodger still owes Neil.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Amount owed} &= 400 - 120 \\\\
&= 280 \text{p} \\\\
&= \pounds \dfrac{280}{100} \\\\
&= \pounds 2.80
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
<div class='working'>

Can we do better than the previous solution? 

If Rodger has paid back $30\%$ of the money, he still has $70\%$ to pay. 
So we can directly calculate the amount owed and save on calculating the amount paid as well.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Amount owed} &= 70\% \text{ of } 400 \\\\
&= \dfrac {70} {100} \times 400 \\\\
&= 70 \times 4 \\\\
&= 280 \text{p} \\\\
&= \pounds \dfrac{280}{100} \\\\
&= \pounds 2.80
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 2.80$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>

Let's convert pounds to pence.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total amount} &= \pounds 4 \\\\
&= 4 \times 100 \text{p} \\\\
&= 400 \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$

Rodger pays back $30 \%$. So,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Amount paid} &= 30\% \text{ of } 400 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30} {100} \times 400 \\\\
&= 30 \times 4 \\\\
&= 120  \text{p} 
\end{aligned}
$

Let's calculate the amount Rodger still owes Neil.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Amount owed} &= 400 - 120 \\\\
&= 280 \text{p} \\\\
&= \pounds \dfrac{280}{100} \\\\
&= \pounds 2.80
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Can we do better than the previous solution? 

If Rodger has paid back $30\%$ of the money, he still has $70\%$ to pay. 
So we can directly calculate the amount owed and save on calculating the amount paid as well.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Amount owed} &= 70\% \text{ of } 400 \\\\
&= \dfrac {70} {100} \times 400 \\\\
&= 70 \times 4 \\\\
&= 280 \text{p} \\\\
&= \pounds \dfrac{280}{100} \\\\
&= \pounds 2.80
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 2.80$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-30031157-8687-4B93-8985-5B4931D1E9A0'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the product of the sum of $8$ and $4$ with the difference of $3$ and $12$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
&= (8 + 4) \times (3 - 12)  \\\\
&= 12 \times (-9) \\\\
&= -108
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$-108$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= (8 + 4) \times (3 - 12)  \\\\
&= 12 \times (-9) \\\\
&= -108
\end{aligned}

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Write down a fraction which is between $\dfrac{1}{3}$ and $\dfrac{1}{2}$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number between $\dfrac{1}{3}$ and $\dfrac{1}{2}$ = $a$

$
\begin{aligned}
a &= Average( \dfrac{1}{3}, \dfrac{1}{2} ) \\\\
  &= ( \dfrac{1}{3} + \dfrac{1}{2} ) \div 2 \\\\
  &= ( \dfrac{1 \times 2}{3 \times 2} + \dfrac{1 \times 3}{2 \times 3} ) \div 2 \\\\
  &= ( \dfrac{2}{6} + \dfrac{3}{6} ) \div 2 \\\\
  &= ( \dfrac{2 + 3}{6}) \div 2 \\\\
  &= ( \dfrac{5}{6}) \div 2 \\\\
  &= \dfrac{5}{6 \times 2} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{5}{12} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
<div class='working'>

TODOWORKING

</div>
</div>

<div class='answer'>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-D0CECCF8-E55D-4AB8-BA2B-E8B0728E9C24'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The $\text{FAB}$ of a number is obtained by multiplying the number by all the whole numbers less than or equal to it down to one.

So $\text{FAB } 3 = 6$ because $3 \times 2 \times 1 = 6$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out $\text{FAB } 5$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {FAB} \ 5   &= 5 \times 4 \times 3 \times 2 \times 1 \\\\
                  &= 120
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$120$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {FAB} \ 5   &= 5 \times 4 \times 3 \times 2 \times 1 \\\\
                  &= 120
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$120$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

If $\text{FAB } x = 720$, what is the value of $x$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

| FAB              |     Value                                       |
|:----------------:|:-----------------------------------------------:|
| $5$              | $5 \times 4 \times 3 \times 2 \times 1 = 120$   |
| $6$              | $120 \times 6 = 720$                            |

$x = 6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>| FAB              |     Value                                       |
|:----------------:|:-----------------------------------------------:|
| $5$              | $5 \times 4 \times 3 \times 2 \times 1 = 120$   |
| $6$              | $120 \times 6 = 720$                            |

$x = 6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is $\text{FAB }11$ divided by $\text{FAB } 9$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {\text {FAB} \ 11} {\text {FAB} \ 9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 \times 10 \times \text {FAB} \ 9} {\text {FAB} \ 9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 \times 10 \times \cancel {\text {FAB} \ 9}} {\cancel {\text {FAB} \ 9}} \\\\
&= 11 \times 10 \\\\
&= 110
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$110$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {\text {FAB} \ 11} {\text {FAB} \ 9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 \times 10 \times \text {FAB} \ 9} {\text {FAB} \ 9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 \times 10 \times \cancel {\text {FAB} \ 9}} {\cancel {\text {FAB} \ 9}} \\\\
&= 11 \times 10 \\\\
&= 110
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-A4EADF93-B120-460C-B5F1-D9351665FB41'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the product of the sum of $6$ and $3$ with the difference between $2$ and $10$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= (6 + 3) \times (2 - 10) \\\\
&= 9 \times (-8) \\\\
&= -72
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$-72$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= (6 + 3) \times (2 - 10) \\\\
&= 9 \times (-8) \\\\
&= -72
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$-72$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-8BA8E32C-E6C1-449D-8EF9-64E872737D42'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the total when you add together all the factors of $72$ (including $1$ and $72$ itself)?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Factors of} \ 72  &= 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 9, 12, 18, 24, 36, 72 \\\\
\text {Sum}              &= 1 + 2 + 3 + 4 + 6 + 8 + 9 + 12 + 18 + 24 + 36 + 72 \\\\
                         &= 195
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$195$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Factors of} \ 72  &= 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 9, 12, 18, 24, 36, 72 \\\\
\text {Sum}              &= 1 + 2 + 3 + 4 + 6 + 8 + 9 + 12 + 18 + 24 + 36 + 72 \\\\
                         &= 195
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$195$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>


<div class='review'>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-051EA915-77B1-4C90-B44F-067124622F8F'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Write $\dfrac{7}{20}$ as a decimal number.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {7} {20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 5} {20 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {35} {100} \\\\
&= 0.35
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.35$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {7} {20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 5} {20 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {35} {100} \\\\
&= 0.35
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class='question question'>

Subtract the difference between $62$ and $35$ from the product of $13$ and $6$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>



$
\begin{aligned}
&= (13 \times 6) - (62 - 35) \\\\
&= 78 - 27 \\\\
&= 51
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$51$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= (13 \times 6) - (62 - 35) \\\\
&= 78 - 27 \\\\
&= 51
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-5D4FC6CE-AFCE-4118-A16D-21C14C304F1B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which sum has been set out correctly to give the answer $67$? 

- $8 + 4 \times 6 - 5$
- $(8 + 4) \times (6 - 5)$
- $(8 + 4) \times 6 - 5$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Option A}
&= 8 + 4 \times 6 - 5 \\\\
&= 8 + 24 - 5 \\\\
&= 32 - 5 \\\\
&= \red
{27} \neq 67
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Option B}     
&= (8 + 4) \times (6 - 5) \\\\
&= 12 \times 1 \\\\
&= \red
{12} \neq 67
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Option C}
&= (8 + 4) \times 6 - 5 \\\\
&= 12 \times 6  - 5 \\\\
&= 72 - 5 \\\\
&= \green
{67}
\end{aligned}
$

Answer $= \text{Option C}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>C</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$(8 + 4) \times 6 - 5$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Option A}
&= 8 + 4 \times 6 - 5 \\\\
&= 8 + 24 - 5 \\\\
&= 32 - 5 \\\\
&= \rtq_katex_color_wrong_answer{27} \neq 67
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Option B}     
&= (8 + 4) \times (6 - 5) \\\\
&= 12 \times 1 \\\\
&= \rtq_katex_color_wrong_answer{12} \neq 67
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Option C}
&= (8 + 4) \times 6 - 5 \\\\
&= 12 \times 6  - 5 \\\\
&= 72 - 5 \\\\
&= \rtq_katex_color_answer{67}
\end{aligned}
$

Answer $= \text{Option C}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>C</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$(8 + 4) \times 6 - 5$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F990AB7B-48EF-4668-8814-D68C1917C568'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Add together the three lengths $1.4 \ \text{m}$, $33 \  \text{cm}$ and $580 \  \text{mm}$ giving your answer in $\text{cm}$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required


$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1.4 \ \text{m} + 33 \ \text{cm} + 580 \ \text{mm} \\\\
&= 1.4 \times 100 \ \text {cm} + 33 \ \text{cm} + \dfrac {580} {10} \ \text{cm} \\\\
&= 140 + 33  + 58 \\\\
&= 231 \ \text{cm}
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$231 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_no_dot_note

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1.4 \ \text{m} + 33 \ \text{cm} + 580 \ \text{mm} \\\\
&= 1.4 \times 100 \ \text {cm} + 33 \ \text{cm} + \dfrac {580} {10} \ \text{cm} \\\\
&= 140 + 33  + 58 \\\\
&= 231 \ \text{cm}
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$231 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

When we multiply three consecutive numbers as shown below we obtain the following answer.

$17 \times 18 \times 19 = 5814$     

**Using this result**, work out the answers to these four questions.

</div>

$
\begin{aligned}
&=17 \times 18 \times 38 \\\\
&=\dfrac{5814}{19} \times 38 \\\\
&=\dfrac{5814\times38}{19}\\\\
&=\dfrac{5814\times19\times2}{19}\\\\
&=\dfrac{5814\times\cancel{19}\times2}{\cancel{19}}\\\\
&=5814\times2 \\\\
&= 11628
\end{aligned}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6692F471-DA27-4415-ADF6-727EF20ED385'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

In a special code, words are replaced by the **product** of the **whole** numbers assigned to the letters. In the code, each letter is given a different number.

For example:- if $\text{S} = 3$, $\text{P} = 4$ and $\text{Y} = 6$ then the word $\text{SPY} = 3 \times 4 \times 6 = 72$

**Using this same method of creating our special code**, work out the answers to the following **four** questions.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

If $\text{TEE} = 20$, find the values of $\text{T}$ and $\text{E}$, if **neither** of the letters has the value $1$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
T \times E \times E &= 20 \\\\
T \times 2E         &= 20 \\\\
T \times E          &= \dfrac{20} {2} \\\\
T \times E          &= \dfrac{10 \times 2} {2} \\\\
T \times E          &= \dfrac{10 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
T \times E          &= 10 \\\\
T \times E          &= 5 \times 2 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

So,

T = $5$

E = $2$

Lets verify,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= T \times E \times E \\\\
&= 5 \times 2 \times 2 \\\\
&= \green
{20} = 20
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$T = 5, E = 2$ 

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
T \times E \times E &= 20 \\\\
T \times 2E         &= 20 \\\\
T \times E          &= \dfrac{20} {2} \\\\
T \times E          &= \dfrac{10 \times 2} {2} \\\\
T \times E          &= \dfrac{10 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
T \times E          &= 10 \\\\
T \times E          &= 5 \times 2 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

So,

T = $5$

E = $2$

Lets verify,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= T \times E \times E \\\\
&= 5 \times 2 \times 2 \\\\
&= \rtq_katex_color_answer{20} = 20
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$T = 5, E = 2$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Then with those values for $\text{T}$ and $\text{E}$, if $\text{TEA} = 70$, find the value of $\text{A}$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
T \times E \times A &= 70 \\\\
5 \times 2 \times A &= 70 \\\\
10 \times A         &= 70 \\\\
A                   &= \dfrac {70} {10} \\\\
A                   &= \dfrac {7 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0}} \\\\
A                   &= 7
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7$

</div>
